## GPS-Related Code Search Results - bGeigieZen Codebase

Based on my thorough search of the bGeigieZen codebase, here are the detailed findings:

### 1. **GPS Library/Module Used**

**Library:** TeenyUbloxConnect (version ^1.0.36)
- Located in: `platformio.ini` line 31
- Library author: beakes
- This is a high-level wrapper library for u-blox GNSS receivers

**Hardware Module:** Beitian BN-880 (u-blox 8 receiver)
- Documented in: `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/bGeigieZen/bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.h` lines 1-8
- Uses UBX (u-blox binary) protocol instead of standard NMEA-0183

### 2. **GPS Initialization and Configuration**

**Main Initialization File:** `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/bGeigieZen/bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.cpp` (930 lines)

**Key Configuration Steps (lines 60-141):**
- Serial connection initialization begins at 38400 baud
- Implements multi-step baud rate detection attempting: 115200 → 38400 → 9600
- Timeouts between attempts: 1000ms, 1200ms, 1200ms respectively
- Calls `_gnss.begin(_serial_conn, timeout)` to initialize the TeenyUbloxConnect library
- Retrieves and logs protocol version information
- Reads current dynamic platform model from GPS

### 3. **Baud Rate and Protocol Details**

**Baud Rates Used (in order of preference):**
- **115200 baud** - Primary attempt
- **38400 baud** - Secondary attempt (also the fallback working rate)
- **9600 baud** - Last resort (default u-blox rate, then switches to 38400)

**Protocol:** UBX (u-blox binary protocol)
- Sync characters: `0xB5 0x62` (defined in `gps_platform_model.h` lines 22-23)
- Disables NMEA-0183 output via: `_gnss.setPortOutput(COM_PORT_UART1, COM_TYPE_UBX)` (line 124)

**Serial Configuration:**
- Uses UART1 as GPS port
- For ESP32-S3: Uses pins RXD2/TXD2 with SERIAL_8N1 format (line 65)
- For other platforms: Uses default serial pins via `Serial2` (line 78 in main.cpp)
- Serial number: `GPS_SERIAL_NUM = 2` (defined in `user_config.h` line 46)

### 4. **GPS-Specific Configuration Commands**

**Sent During Activation (lines 123-138):**
```
1. setPortOutput(COM_PORT_UART1, COM_TYPE_UBX)  - Enable UBX only
2. setAutoNAVPVT(true)                          - Enable position/velocity/time auto-reporting (1 second updates)
3. setAutoNAVSAT(false)                         - Disable by default (navsat worker handles separately)
4. restoreGpsMemoryFromNVS()                    - Restore from ESP32 non-volatile storage
5. restoreDatabaseFromSD()                      - Load GNSS database from SD card
6. injectWarmStartSeedFromSD()                  - Load warm-start seed for faster fix
```

**Dynamic Model Configuration:** (lines 144-158, 547-582)
- Supports setting u-blox dynamic platform models via UBX-CFG-VALSET message
- Models available: PORTABLE, STATIONARY, PEDESTRIAN, AUTOMOTIVE, SEA, AIRBORNE_1G, AIRBORNE_2G, AIRBORNE_4G, WRIST
- Aliases defined: `DYNMODEL_PORT` and `DYNMODEL_AIR4`

**Data Collection:**
- Main data: UBX-NAV-PVT (Position, Velocity, Time in one message)
- Additional: UBX-NAV-SAT (via separate NavsatCollector worker) at 10Hz rate
- Extracts: position (lat/lon), altitude MSL, heading, velocity, accuracy estimates, satellites in fix

**Advanced Features:**
- UBX-MGA-DBD database dump/restore for faster acquisition
- UBX-RXM-PMREQ backup mode for power-down state preservation
- UBX-MGA-INI-TIME_UTC and UBX-MGA-INI-POS_LLH for warm-start injection
- Fix age monitoring: data marked stale after 4500ms (defined in `user_config.h` line 47)

1	/** @brief GNSS handler using UBX protocol library 
2	 * 
3	 * When activated, set auto receive for the UBX protocol commands used:
4	 * UBX-NAV-PVT: position, velocity and time
5	 * ***REMOVED*** UBX-NAV-DOP: dilution of precision, for horizontal DOP
6	 * ***REMOVED*** UBX-NAV-SAT: enumeration of satellites in view, for number of sats.
7	 * Based on examples by Paul Clark, SparkFun Electronics
8	 * https://github.com/sparkfun/SparkFun_u-blox_GNSS_Arduino_Library
9	 * 
10	 * ***REMOVED*** The two removed packets were not providing any additional
11	 * useful information. HDOP was always zero. NSATS was the total number of
12	 * satellites, regardless of their status in computing a fix.
13	*/

60	bool GpsConnector::activate(bool retry) {
61	  // From Sparkfun examples/Example12_UseUart
62	  // Assume that the U-Blox GNSS is running at 9600 baud (the default) or at 38400 baud.
63	  if (!retry) {
64	#ifdef CONFIG_IDF_TARGET_ESP32S3 // Core S3SE fix using the correct serial pins
65	    _serial_conn.begin(38400, SERIAL_8N1, RXD2, TXD2);
66	#else
67	    _serial_conn.begin(38400);
68	#endif
69	
70	    _init_at = millis();
71	  }
72	  if (_tried_115200_at == 0 && (millis() - _init_at > 1000)) { // Wait for device to completely startup
73	    _tried_115200_at = millis();
74	    _serial_conn.updateBaudRate(115200);
75	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Try at 115200 baud");
76	    if (_gnss.begin(_serial_conn, 500)) {
77	      M5_LOGD("GNSS: connected at 115200 baud"); // no need to set module to 38.4
78	    }
79	    else {
80	      return false;
81	    }
82	  }
83	  else if (_tried_38400_at == 0 && _tried_115200_at > 0 && (millis() - _tried_115200_at > 1200)) { // Wait for device to completely startup
84	    _tried_38400_at = millis();
85	    _serial_conn.updateBaudRate(38400);
86	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Try at 38400 baud");
87	    if (_gnss.begin(_serial_conn, 500)) {
88	      M5_LOGD("GNSS: connected at 38400 baud"); // no need to set module to 38.4
89	    }
90	    else {
91	      return false;
92	    }
93	  }
94	  else if (_tried_9600_at == 0 && _tried_38400_at > 0 && (millis() - _tried_38400_at > 1200)) {
95	    _tried_9600_at = millis();
96	    _serial_conn.updateBaudRate(9600);
97	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Try at 9600 baud");
98	    if (_gnss.begin(_serial_conn, 1000)) {
99	      M5_LOGD("GNSS: connected at 9600 baud, switching to 38400");
100	      _gnss.setSerialRate(38400);
101	      delay(100); // recovery time for gnss module baud rate change
102	      _serial_conn.updateBaudRate(38400);
103	    }
104	    else {
105	      return false;
106	    }
107	  }
108	  else {
109	    return false;
110	  }
111	
112	  // Confirm that we actually have a connection
113	  data.protocolVersionHigh = _gnss.getProtocolVersionHigh();
114	  data.protocolVersionLow = _gnss.getProtocolVersionLow();
115	  M5_LOGD("GNSS: u-blox protocol version %02d.%02d",
116	              data.protocolVersionHigh, data.protocolVersionLow);
117	              
118	  // Check the current dynamic model on startup
119	  // This will help us verify if the setting persisted after power cycling
120	  M5_LOGD("GNSS: Reading current dynamic model...");
121	  readDynamicModelFromGPS();
122	
123	  // Send UBX, disable NMEA-0183 messages that we are ignoring anyway.
124	  _gnss.setPortOutput(COM_PORT_UART1, COM_TYPE_UBX);
125	
126	  // Set Auto on NAV-PVT for non-blocking access
127	  // getPVT() will return true if a new navigation solution is available
128	  _gnss.setAutoNAVPVT(true); // Tell the GNSS to send the solution as it is computed (1 second)
129	  _gnss.setAutoNAVSAT(false); // Disable navsat by default (navsat worker handles this)
130	
131	  // Announce/perform restore path on boot (relies on internal backup domain)
132	  restoreGpsMemoryFromNVS();
133	
134	  // Try to restore full database from SD if available
135	  restoreDatabaseFromSD();
136	
137	  // Try to inject warm-start seed from SD if present
138	  injectWarmStartSeedFromSD();
139	
140	  return true;
141	}

1	/** @brief Manage position fix and date-time from GNSS receiver. 
2	 * 
3	 * Based on u-blox 8 receiver integrated Beitian BN-880 module, using the UBX communications
4	 * protocol instead of the usual NMEA-183. The problem with NMEA is that it was really 
5	 * designed for boats, where close enough to see with binoculars was good enough (well, not
6	 * really but it makes for a good story). The real issue is that it's hard to get consistent
7	 * fix and time data by parsing NMEA sentences.
8	 * 
9	*/
10	
11	#ifndef BGEIGIEZEN_GPS_SENSOR_H_
12	#define BGEIGIEZEN_GPS_SENSOR_H_
13	
14	#include <Arduino.h>
15	#include <RBD_Timer.h>
16	#include <Worker.hpp>
17	#include <user_config.h>
18	
19	#include <TeenyUbloxConnect.h>
20	#include "gps_platform_model.h"
21	
22	struct GnssData {
23	  // When true, the item related to each Boolean is valid and updated in the
24	  // most recent poll of the gps_connector worker by the controller. If the 
25	  // gps_connector doesn't have a new fix because it only becomes available
26	  // every 1 second, then the corresponding Boolean becomes false.
27	  // If stale, render in gray/white text. 
28	  bool location_valid;
29	  bool date_valid;
30	  bool time_valid;
31	
32	  bool valid() const {
33	    return location_valid && date_valid && time_valid;
34	  }
35	
36	  enum Heading {
37	    UNKNOWN,
38	    NORTH,
39	    NORTHEAST,
40	    EAST,
41	    SOUTHEAST,
42	    SOUTH,
43	    SOUTHWEST,
44	    WEST,
45	    NORTHWEST,
46	  };
47	
48	  // Position
49	  double latitude;  // Longitude: deg
50	  double longitude; // Longitude: deg
51	  double altitudeMSL;  // above mean sea level in meters
52	  double heading_degree; // in degrees
53	  Heading heading;
54	
55	  // Confidence
56	  uint8_t satsInView;  // (NavSat) satellites used to calculate fix
57	  uint8_t  numSV;  // (NavPvt) Number of satellites used in Nav Solution
58	  double pdop;  // position dilution of precision (not horizontal)
59	  uint32_t hAcc;  // mm Horizontal accuracy estimate for Long/Lat
60	  uint32_t vAcc;  // mm Vertical accuracy estimate for Long/Lat
61	  int32_t  velN;  // mm/s NED north velocity
62	  int32_t  velE;  // mm/s NED east velocity
63	  int32_t  velD;  // mm/s NED down velocity
64	  int32_t  gSpeed;  // Ground Speed (2-D)
65	  int32_t  headMot;  // Heading of motion (2-D)
66	  uint32_t sAcc;
67	  uint32_t headAcc;
68	  bool invalidLlh;  // NAVPVT[78] flags3 bit 0
69	
70	
71	  // Date & Time
72	  uint16_t year;
73	  uint8_t month;
74	  uint8_t day;
75	  uint8_t hour;
76	  uint8_t minute;
77	  uint8_t second;
78	
79	  // Debug info
80	  uint8_t protocolVersionHigh;
81	  uint8_t protocolVersionLow;
82	
83	};
84	
85	/**
86	 * GPS device worker, produces the current GPS location.
87	 */
88	class GpsConnector : public Worker<GnssData> {
89	 public:
90	
91	  explicit GpsConnector(TeenyUbloxConnect& _gnss, HardwareSerial& serial);
92	
93	  virtual ~GpsConnector() = default;
94	  
95	  
96	  /**
97	   * Backup U-blox GPS memory (almanac, ephemeris, satellite data) to M5Stack NVS
98	   * @return true if backup was successful
99	   */
100	  bool backupGpsMemoryToNVS();
101	  
102	  /**
103	   * Restore U-blox GPS memory from M5Stack NVS for faster startup
104	   * @return true if restore was successful
105	   */
106	  bool restoreGpsMemoryFromNVS();
107	
108	  /**
109	   * Save a warm-start seed (time/position) to SD card for faster startup without VBAT.
110	   * File path: /gnss/warm_seed.bin
111	   * @return true if file written
112	   */
113	  bool saveWarmStartSeedToSD();
114	
115	  /**
116	   * Load warm-start seed from SD card and prepare for injection.
117	   * Current implementation validates and logs the seed.
118	   * @return true if seed loaded
119	   */
120	  bool injectWarmStartSeedFromSD();
121	
122	  /**
123	   * Dump the GNSS database (UBX-MGA-DBD) to SD card.
124	   * File path: /gnss/dbd_latest.bin (raw UBX frames)
125	   */
126	  bool dumpDatabaseToSD();
127	
128	  /**
129	   * Restore the GNSS database from SD card by replaying stored UBX frames.
130	   * File path: /gnss/dbd_latest.bin
131	   */
132	  bool restoreDatabaseFromSD();
133	
134	  bool activate(bool retry) override;
135	
136	  int8_t produce_data() override;
137	
138	  /**
139	   * Set the GPS dynamic platform model
140	   * @param model The dynamic model to set (e.g., DYNMODEL_PORT, DYNMODEL_AIR4)
141	   * @param saveToFlash If true, saves the setting to flash memory so it persists after power cycles
142	   * @return true if successful, false otherwise
143	   */
144	  bool setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model, bool saveToFlash);
145	  
146	  /**
147	   * Set the GPS dynamic platform model (RAM only version)
148	   * @param model The dynamic model to set (e.g., DYNMODEL_PORT, DYNMODEL_AIR4)
149	   * @return true if successful, false otherwise
150	   */
151	  bool setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model);
152	  
153	  /**
154	   * Get the current GPS dynamic platform model
155	   * @return The current dynamic model
156	   */
157	  UbxDynamicModel getDynamicModel();
158	  
159	  /**
160	   * Read the current dynamic model directly from the GPS module
161	   * @return true if successful, false otherwise
162	   */
163	  bool readDynamicModelFromGPS();
164	  
165	  /**
166	   * Send a raw UBX message to the GPS module
167	   * @param msgClass UBX message class
168	   * @param msgID UBX message ID
169	   * @param payload Payload data
170	   * @param payloadSize Size of the payload
171	   * @return true if successful, false otherwise
172	   */
173	  bool sendUBXMessage(uint8_t msgClass, uint8_t msgID, const uint8_t* payload, size_t payloadSize);
174	  /**
175	   * Request the GNSS module to enter Backup mode and save its state.
176	   * Uses UBX-RXM-PMREQ with flags = 0x00000001.
177	   * Should be called just before power is removed.
178	   * @return true if the UBX frame was sent.
179	   */
180	  bool requestBackup();
181	  
182	  /**
183	   * Calculate UBX message checksum
184	   * @param data Data to calculate checksum for
185	   * @param len Length of data
186	   * @param cka Pointer to store CK_A
187	   * @param ckb Pointer to store CK_B
188	   */
189	  void calculateChecksum(const uint8_t* data, size_t len, uint8_t* cka, uint8_t* ckb);
190	
191	 protected:
192	  void deactivate() override;
193	
194	 private:
195	  /* u-blox UBX protocol query PVT gets position, velocity & time in one call.
196	   * The call returns false if no new fix has been received. In other words,
197	   * each call only succeeds once until the next fix update.
198	   * Use pdop and satsInView to determine acceptability.
199	  */
200	  TeenyUbloxConnect& _gnss;
201	  HardwareSerial& _serial_conn;
202	  uint32_t _tried_115200_at;
203	  uint32_t _tried_38400_at;
204	  uint32_t _tried_9600_at;
205	  uint32_t _init_at;
206	
207	  double _last_latitude;
208	  double _last_longitude;
209	  
210	  // Current GPS dynamic model
211	  UbxDynamicModel _current_model = DYNMODEL_PORT;
212	
213	  // Age each item. If the corresponding timer times out, it's stale.
214	  RBD::Timer location_timer{GPS_FIX_AGE_LIMIT};
215	  RBD::Timer date_timer{GPS_FIX_AGE_LIMIT};
216	  RBD::Timer time_timer{GPS_FIX_AGE_LIMIT};
217	  RBD::Timer time_getpvt{GPS_FIX_AGE_LIMIT};  // if no response from getPVT()
218	};

540	/**
541	 * Set the GPS dynamic platform model
542	 * @param model The dynamic model to set (e.g., DYNMODEL_PORT, DYNMODEL_AIR4)
543	 * @param saveToFlash If true, saves the setting to flash memory so it persists after power cycles
544	 * @return true if successful, false otherwise
545	 */
546	bool GpsConnector::setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model, bool saveToFlash) {
547	  // Implementation based on u-blox M10 interface description (UBX-21035062)
548	  bool success = false;
549	  
550	  // Log the changes
551	  Serial.printf("Setting GPS dynamic model to %d (saveToFlash: %d)\n", model, saveToFlash);
552	  M5_LOGD("GNSS: Setting dynamic model to %d (saveToFlash: %d)", model, saveToFlash);
553	  
554	  // Create UBX-CFG-VALSET message payload
555	  uint8_t msgPayload[12]; // UBX-CFG-VALSET message payload
556	  
557	  // Header: version, layer, reserved
558	  msgPayload[0] = 0x00; // version 0
559	  msgPayload[1] = saveToFlash ? UBX_CFG_LAYER_ALL : UBX_CFG_LAYER_RAM; // layers: RAM only or RAM+BBR+Flash
560	  msgPayload[2] = 0x00; // reserved
561	  msgPayload[3] = 0x00; // reserved
562	  
563	  // Key: CFG-NAVSPG-DYNMODEL (0x20110021)
564	  msgPayload[4] = 0x21; // LSB
565	  msgPayload[5] = 0x00;
566	  msgPayload[6] = 0x11;
567	  msgPayload[7] = 0x20; // MSB
568	  
569	  // Value: dynamic model (1 byte, padded to 4 bytes)
570	  msgPayload[8] = (uint8_t)model; // Dynamic model value
571	  msgPayload[9] = 0x00; // padding
572	  msgPayload[10] = 0x00; // padding
573	  msgPayload[11] = 0x00; // padding
574	  
575	  // Send the UBX-CFG-VALSET message directly using our custom method
576	  if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_CFG_VALSET, msgPayload, sizeof(msgPayload))) {
577	    // Wait a bit for the message to be processed
578	    delay(100);
579	    
580	    // Store the current model
581	    _current_model = model;
582	    success = true;
583	    
584	    // Log the model that was set
585	    const char* modelName = "UNKNOWN";
586	    switch(model) {
587	      case DYNMODEL_PORT: modelName = "PORTABLE"; break;
588	      case DYNMODEL_STATIONARY: modelName = "STATIONARY"; break;
589	      case DYNMODEL_PEDESTRIAN: modelName = "PEDESTRIAN"; break;
590	      case DYNMODEL_AUTOMOTIVE: modelName = "AUTOMOTIVE"; break;
591	      case DYNMODEL_SEA: modelName = "SEA"; break;
592	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_1G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 1G"; break;
593	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_2G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 2G"; break;
594	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_4G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 4G"; break;
595	      case DYNMODEL_WRIST: modelName = "WRIST"; break;
596	    }
597	    
598	    Serial.printf("GPS set to %s mode\n", modelName);
599	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Set to %s mode (saveToFlash: %d)", modelName, saveToFlash);
600	    
601	    // Save to NVS for persistence across power cycles
602	    // Note: We need access to LocalStorage instance to save this
603	    // This will be handled by the calling code that has access to LocalStorage
604	    
605	  } else {
606	    Serial.println("Failed to send dynamic model change command");
607	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model change command");
608	  }
609	  
610	  return success;
611	}

618	bool GpsConnector::setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model) {

632	/**
633	 * Read the current dynamic model directly from the GPS module
634	 * @return true if successful, false otherwise
635	 */
636	bool GpsConnector::readDynamicModelFromGPS() {
637	  bool success = false;
638	  
639	  // Create UBX-CFG-VALGET message payload
640	  uint8_t msgPayload[8];
641	  
642	  // Header: version, layer, reserved
643	  msgPayload[0] = 0x00; // version 0
644	  msgPayload[1] = UBX_CFG_LAYER_RAM; // layer: RAM
645	  msgPayload[2] = 0x00; // reserved
646	  msgPayload[3] = 0x00; // reserved
647	  
648	  // Key: CFG-NAVSPG-DYNMODEL (0x20110021)
649	  msgPayload[4] = 0x21; // LSB
650	  msgPayload[5] = 0x00;
651	  msgPayload[6] = 0x11;
652	  msgPayload[7] = 0x20; // MSB
653	  
654	  // Send the UBX-CFG-VALGET message
655	  if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_CFG_VALGET, msgPayload, sizeof(msgPayload))) {
656	    // Wait for response
657	    delay(100);
658	    
659	    // In a real implementation, we would parse the response packet
660	    // For now, we'll just use our stored value and log it
661	    
662	    // Map the model to a string for better readability
663	    const char* modelName = "UNKNOWN";
664	    switch(_current_model) {
665	      case DYNMODEL_PORT: modelName = "PORTABLE"; break;
666	      case DYNMODEL_STATIONARY: modelName = "STATIONARY"; break;
667	      case DYNMODEL_PEDESTRIAN: modelName = "PEDESTRIAN"; break;
668	      case DYNMODEL_AUTOMOTIVE: modelName = "AUTOMOTIVE"; break;
669	      case DYNMODEL_SEA: modelName = "SEA"; break;
670	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_1G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 1G"; break;
671	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_2G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 2G"; break;
672	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_4G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 4G"; break;
673	      case DYNMODEL_WRIST: modelName = "WRIST"; break;
674	    }
675	    
676	    Serial.printf("Current GPS dynamic model: %s (%d)\n", modelName, _current_model);
677	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Current dynamic model: %s (%d)", modelName, _current_model);
678	    
679	    success = true;
680	  } else {
681	    Serial.println("Failed to send dynamic model query command");
682	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model query command");
683	  }
684	  
685	  return success;
686	}

688	
689	int8_t GpsConnector::produce_data() {
690	  auto ret_status = e_worker_idle;
691	
692	  // getPVT returns true if there is a fresh navigation solution available.
693	  // "LLH" is longitude, latitude, height.
694	  // getPVT() returns UTC date and time.
695	  // Do not use GNSS time, see u-blox spec section 9.
696	  if (_gnss.getNAVPVT()) {
697	    // M5_LOGD("[%d] _gnss.getPVT() is true.", millis());
698	
699	    data.satsInView = _gnss.getNumSV(); // Satellites In View (numSV)
700	
701	    if (_gnss.getFixType() == 2 || _gnss.getFixType() == 3) {
702	      // M5_LOGD("[%d] fix type is 2D or 3D.", millis());
703	      data.pdop = _gnss.getPDOP() * 1e-2; // Position Dilution of Precision
704	      _last_latitude = data.latitude;
705	      _last_longitude = data.longitude;
706	      data.latitude = _gnss.getLatitude() * 1e-7;
707	      data.longitude = _gnss.getLongitude() * 1e-7;
708	      data.heading_degree = _gnss.getHeading() * 1e-5;
709	      if (data.heading_degree >= NNW || data.heading_degree < NNE) {
710	        data.heading = GnssData::NORTH;
711	      }
712	      if (data.heading_degree >= NNE || data.heading_degree < ENE) {
713	        data.heading = GnssData::NORTHEAST;
714	      }
715	      if (data.heading_degree >= ENE || data.heading_degree < ESE) {
716	        data.heading = GnssData::EAST;
717	      }
718	      if (data.heading_degree >= ESE || data.heading_degree < SSE) {
719	        data.heading = GnssData::SOUTHEAST;
720	      }
721	      if (data.heading_degree >= SSE || data.heading_degree < SSW) {
722	        data.heading = GnssData::SOUTH;
723	      }
724	      if (data.heading_degree >= SSW || data.heading_degree < WSW) {
725	        data.heading = GnssData::SOUTHWEST;
726	      }
727	      if (data.heading_degree >= WSW || data.heading_degree < WNW) {
728	        data.heading = GnssData::WEST;
729	      }
730	      if (data.heading_degree >= WNW || data.heading_degree < NNW) {
731	        data.heading = GnssData::NORTHWEST;
732	      }
733	      data.altitudeMSL = _gnss.getFixType() == 3 ? _gnss.getAltitudeMSL() * 1e-3 : 0; // Above MSL (not ellipsoid)
734	      const auto distance_step = haversine_km(data.latitude, data.longitude, _last_latitude, _last_longitude);
735	
736	
737	      // Retrieve extra confidence indicators from NavPvt
738	      data.hAcc = _gnss.getHorizontalAccEst();  // mm Horizontal accuracy estimate for Long/Lat
739	      data.vAcc = _gnss.getVerticalAccEst();  // mm Vertical accuracy estimate for Long/Lat
740	      data.velN = _gnss.getVelN();  // mm/s NED north velocity
741	      data.velE = _gnss.getVelE();  // mm/s NED east velocity
742	      data.velD = _gnss.getVelD();  // mm/s NED down velocity
743	      data.gSpeed = _gnss.getGroundSpeed();  // Ground Speed (2-D)
744	      data.headMot = _gnss.getHeading();  // Heading of motion (2-D)
745	      data.sAcc = _gnss.getSpeedAccEst();
746	      data.headAcc = _gnss.getHeadingAccEst();
747	
748	      data.location_valid = _gnss.getGnssFixOk() && distance_step < 0.5; // Airplanes fly at 255m per second, 500 meter check is good enough
749	      location_timer.restart();
750	      ret_status = e_worker_data_read;
751	      // DEBUG: Compare PDOP from NAV-PVT and HDOP from NAV-DOP
752	      // M5_LOGD("[%d] GnssFixOk (type = %d)."
753	      //               "  SATS: %d; PDOP: %d; HDOP: %d\n",
754	      //               millis(), _gnss.getFixType(),
755	      //               data.satsInView, _gnss.getPDOP(), data.hdop);
756	    }
757	
758	    if (_gnss.getDateValid()) {
759	//      M5_LOGD("[%lu] _gnss.getDateValid() is true.", millis());
760	      data.year = _gnss.getYear();
761	      data.month = _gnss.getMonth();
762	      data.day = _gnss.getDay();
763	      data.date_valid = true;
764	      date_timer.restart();
765	      ret_status = e_worker_data_read;
766	    }
767	
768	    if (_gnss.getTimeValid()) {
769	//      M5_LOGD("[%lu] _gnss.getTimeValid() is true.", millis());
770	      data.hour = _gnss.getHour();
771	      data.minute = _gnss.getMinute();
772	      data.second = _gnss.getSecond();
773	      data.time_valid = true;
774	      time_timer.restart();
775	      ret_status = e_worker_data_read;
776	    }
777	  }
778	
779	  // Check expiry
780	  if (location_timer.isExpired()) {
781	    data.location_valid = false;
782	  }
783	  if (time_timer.isExpired()) {
784	    data.time_valid = false;
785	    data.hour = GPS_INVALID_HOUR;
786	    data.minute = GPS_INVALID_MINUTE;
787	    data.second = GPS_INVALID_SECOND;
788	  }
789	  if (date_timer.isExpired()) {
790	    data.date_valid = false;
791	    data.year = GPS_INVALID_YEAR;
792	    data.month = GPS_INVALID_MONTH;
793	    data.day = GPS_INVALID_DAY;
794	  }
795	
796	  ApiDataCache::instance().update(data);
797	  return ret_status;
798	}
799	
800	/**
801	 * Send a raw UBX message to the GPS module
802	 * @param msgClass UBX message class
803	 * @param msgID UBX message ID
804	 * @param payload Payload data
805	 * @param payloadSize Size of the payload
806	 * @return true if successful, false otherwise
807	 */
808	bool GpsConnector::sendUBXMessage(uint8_t msgClass, uint8_t msgID, const uint8_t* payload, size_t payloadSize) {
809	  // UBX message structure:
810	  // Sync Char 1: 0xB5
811	  // Sync Char 2: 0x62
812	  // Class: 1 byte
813	  // ID: 1 byte
814	  // Length: 2 bytes (little endian)
815	  // Payload: variable length
816	  // Checksum: 2 bytes (CK_A, CK_B)
817	  
818	  // Write sync chars
819	  _serial_conn.write(UBX_SYNC_CHAR_1);
820	  _serial_conn.write(UBX_SYNC_CHAR_2);
821	  
822	  // Write class and ID
823	  _serial_conn.write(msgClass);
824	  _serial_conn.write(msgID);
825	  
826	  // Write length (little endian)
827	  _serial_conn.write(payloadSize & 0xFF);
828	  _serial_conn.write((payloadSize >> 8) & 0xFF);
829	  
830	  // Write payload
831	  _serial_conn.write(payload, payloadSize);
832	  
833	  // Calculate checksum
834	  uint8_t ck_a = 0, ck_b = 0;
835	  
836	  // Add class, ID, and length to checksum
837	  ck_a += msgClass;
838	  ck_b += ck_a;
839	  
840	  ck_a += msgID;
841	  ck_b += ck_a;
842	  
843	  ck_a += payloadSize & 0xFF;
844	  ck_b += ck_a;
845	  
846	  ck_a += (payloadSize >> 8) & 0xFF;
847	  ck_b += ck_a;
848	  
849	  // Add payload to checksum
850	  for (size_t i = 0; i < payloadSize; i++) {
851	    ck_a += payload[i];
852	    ck_b += ck_a;
853	  }
854	  
855	  // Write checksum
856	  _serial_conn.write(ck_a);
857	  _serial_conn.write(ck_b);
858	  
859	  // Flush the serial buffer
860	  _serial_conn.flush();
861	  
862	  // Wait a bit for the message to be processed
863	  delay(5);
864	  
865	  return true;
866	}
867	
868	/**
869	 * Calculate UBX message checksum
870	 * @param data Data to calculate checksum for
871	 * @param len Length of data
872	 * @param cka Pointer to store CK_A
873	 * @param ckb Pointer to store CK_B
874	 */
875	void GpsConnector::calculateChecksum(const uint8_t* data, size_t len, uint8_t* cka, uint8_t* ckb) {
876	  *cka = 0;
877	  *ckb = 0;
878	  
879	  for (size_t i = 0; i < len; i++) {
880	    *cka += data[i];
881	    *ckb += *cka;
882	  }
883	}
884	
885	/**
886	 * Ask the GNSS to enter Backup mode (save state) using UBX-RXM-PMREQ.
887	 * This dramatically reduces TTFF on the next boot.
888	 */
889	bool GpsConnector::requestBackup() {
890	  // UBX-RXM-PMREQ (0x02 0x41) payload is 8 bytes:
891	  // 0-1: version / reserved (set to 0)
892	  // 2-3: duration (little-endian) – 0 means indefinite
893	  // 4-7: flags – bit0 = backup, everything else 0
894	  const uint8_t payload[8] = { 0x00, 0x00, // version / reserved
895	                               0x00, 0x00, // duration LSB/MSB (0 = indefinitely)
896	                               0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00 }; // flags (backup)
897	  return sendUBXMessage(0x02 /*RXM*/, 0x41 /*PMREQ*/, payload, sizeof(payload));
898	}
899

904	  // This is a simplified implementation - full implementation would need
905	  // to handle multiple UBX-MGA message types and responses
906	  
907	  // For now, we'll use the existing backup functionality which saves
908	  // the GPS state to its internal backup memory

910	  
911	  if (success) {
912	    M5_LOGI("GPS: Memory backup completed - GPS internal state saved");
913	  } else {
914	    M5_LOGE("GPS: Memory backup failed");
915	  }
916	  
917	  return success;
918	}

21	/********************************************************************/
22	// GNSS Satellite Key
23	/********************************************************************/
24	// GNSS      gnssId  Type  #ofSats  isMajor  Ublox_M8_Compatible
25	// ----      ------  ----  -------  -------  -------------------
26	// GPS       0       G     31       YES      YES
27	// SBAS      1       S     WEB      NO       YES
28	// Galileo   2       E     27       YES      YES
29	// BeiDou    3       B     44       YES      YES
30	// IMES      4       I     0        NO       YES
31	// QZSS      5       Q     4        NO       YES
32	// GLONASS   6       R     24       YES      YES
33	// NAVIC     7       N     5        ?        NO
34	/********************************************************************/
35	// Ublox M8 GNSS Configuration
36	/********************************************************************/
37	// The Ublox 8 series (M8) supports reception from satellites on 3
38	// different frequency bands:
39	//   a) GPS+SBAS+GALILEO+QZSS (L1)
40	//   b) GLONASS (L1)
41	//   c) BEIDOU (B1I)
42	// ...BUT...
43	// M8 can only process 2 frequency bands concurrently.
44	// ...So...
45	// You can't enable GPS, GLONASS and BEIDOU at the same time (and the
46	// M8 module wouldn't let you if you tried).
47	//
48	// You need to choose which 2 (or 1) out of 3 groups (a, b, c) to
49	// enable in your M8 module.
50	// Notes:
51	// - Choosing your major GNSS (GPS, Galileo, BeiDou, GLONASS) should
52	//   based on your region.
53	// - Minor GNSS (SBAS, QZSS) choice should also be region-based.
54	//
55	// Example opinion from 'clive1' on ublox forum regarding M8 GNSS
56	// configuration for CONUS (continental US) with emphasis on power savings:
57	// - I wouldn't bother tracking QZSS within the CONUS, just grinding gears.
58	// - I'd suppose having many constellations to speed TTFF and your
59	//   ability to turn-off the receiver more quickly is where energy
60	//   would get saved. GALILEO tends to be slow to acquire on these designs.
61	// - Only SBAS covers CONUS, SBAS Only provides data for GPS Satellites
62	// - Skeptical of the overall value of SBAS in this scenario.
63	// - I'd perhaps lean to GPS+GLO.
64	
65	/********************************************************************/
66	// UBX Receiver Types
67	/********************************************************************/
68	enum ubx_module_type_t : uint8_t {
69	  UBLOX_UNKNOWN_MODULE = 0,
70	  UBLOX_M8_MODULE      = 8,
71	  UBLOX_M9_MODULE      = 9,
72	  UBLOX_M10_MODULE     = 10
73	};
74	
75	/********************************************************************/
76	// UBX Packet Frame Defines
77	/********************************************************************/
78	const uint8_t  COM_PORT_UART1 = 1;
79	const uint8_t  COM_TYPE_UBX  = 0x01;
80	const uint8_t  COM_TYPE_NMEA = 0x02;
81	const uint8_t  UBX_MAXNAVSATSATELLITES = 64; // Max payload determined by NAV-SAT max satellites
82	const uint16_t UBX_MAXPAYLOADLENGTH = 8 + (12 * (uint16_t)UBX_MAXNAVSATSATELLITES);
83	const uint8_t  UBX_SYNCH_1 = 0xB5;
84	const uint8_t  UBX_SYNCH_2 = 0x62;
85	const uint8_t  UBX_CLASS_NAV = 0x01;
86	const uint8_t    UBX_NAV_STATUS = 0x03;
87	const uint16_t   UBX_NAV_STATUS_PAYLOADLENGTH = 16;
88	const uint16_t   UBX_NAV_STATUS_PACKETLENGTH = 24;
89	const uint8_t    UBX_NAV_PVT    = 0x07;

91	const uint16_t   UBX_NAV_PVT_PACKETLENGTH = 100;
92	const uint8_t    UBX_NAV_SAT    = 0x35;
93	const uint16_t   UBX_NAV_SAT_MINPAYLOADLENGTH = 8;

95	const uint16_t   UBX_NAV_SAT_MAXPAYLOADLENGTH = UBX_MAXPAYLOADLENGTH;
96	const uint16_t   UBX_NAV_SAT_MAXPACKETLENGTH = UBX_NAV_SAT_MAXPAYLOADLENGTH + 8;
97	const uint8_t  UBX_CLASS_ACK = 0x05;

29	/********************************************************************/
30	/********************************************************************/
31	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::begin(Stream &serialPort_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
32	  serialPort = &serialPort_;
33	  resetNAVSTATUSInfo(); // reset spoofing flags
34	  ubloxModuleType = UBLOX_UNKNOWN_MODULE;
35	  ubloxCFGGNSSInfoValid = false;
36	  // Try three times
37	  bool connected = false;
38	  if(pollUART1Port_M8(maxWait_, true)) {
39	    connected = true;
40	  } else if(pollUART1Port_M8(maxWait_, true)) {
41	    connected = true;
42	  } else if(pollUART1Port_M8(maxWait_, true)) {
43	    connected = true;
44	  }
45	  if(connected) {
46	    if(pollProtocolVersion(maxWait_)) {
47	      if((protocolVersionHigh == 18) && (protocolVersionLow == 00)) {
48	        ubloxModuleType = UBLOX_M8_MODULE;
49	        return true;
50	      } else if((protocolVersionHigh == 32) && (protocolVersionLow == 01)) {
51	        ubloxModuleType = UBLOX_M9_MODULE;
52	        return true;
53	      } else if((protocolVersionHigh == 34) && (protocolVersionLow == 10)) {
54	        ubloxModuleType = UBLOX_M10_MODULE;
55	        return true;
56	      }
57	    }
58	  }
59	  return false;
60	}
61	
62	/********************************************************************/
63	/********************************************************************/
64	// ublox commands
65	/********************************************************************/
66	/********************************************************************/
67	uint8_t TeenyUbloxConnect::getUbloxModuleType() {
68	  return ubloxModuleType;
69	}
70	
71	/********************************************************************/
72	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::pollUART1Port(uint16_t maxWait_) {
73	  if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M8_MODULE) {
74	    return pollUART1Port_M8(maxWait_);
75	  } else if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M9_MODULE) {
76	    return pollUART1Port_M10(maxWait_);
77	  } else if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M10_MODULE) {
78	    return pollUART1Port_M10(maxWait_);
79	  }
80	  return false;
81	}
82	/********************************************************************/
83	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::pollUART1Port_M8(uint16_t maxWait_, bool flushPort_) {
84	  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;
85	  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_PRT;
86	  commandPacket.payloadLength = 1;
87	  commandPacket.payload[0] = COM_PORT_UART1;
88	  commandPacket.validPacket = true;
89	  if(flushPort_) while(serialPort->available()) serialPort->read();
90	  return sendCommandPacket(true, true, maxWait_);
91	}
92	/********************************************************************/
93	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::pollUART1Port_M10(uint16_t maxWait_, bool flushPort_) {
94	  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;
95	  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_VALGET;
96	  commandPacket.payloadLength = 8;
97	  commandPacket.payload[0] = 0;
98	  commandPacket.payload[1] = VALGET_LAYER_RAM;
99	  commandPacket.payload[2] = 0;
100	  commandPacket.payload[3] = 0;
101	  for(uint8_t i = 0; i < 4; i++)
102	    commandPacket.payload[4 + i] = UBLOX_CFG_UART1_ENABLED >> (8 * i);
103	  commandPacket.validPacket = true;
104	  if(flushPort_) while(serialPort->available()) serialPort->read();
105	  return sendCommandPacket(true, true, maxWait_);
106	}
107	
108	/********************************************************************/
109	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::setPortOutput(uint8_t portID_, uint8_t comSettings_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
110	  if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M8_MODULE) {
111	    return setPortOutput_M8(portID_, comSettings_, maxWait_);
112	  } else if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M9_MODULE) {
113	    return setPortOutput_M10(portID_, comSettings_, maxWait_);
114	  } else if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M10_MODULE) {
115	    return setPortOutput_M10(portID_, comSettings_, maxWait_);
116	  }
117	  return false;
118	}
119	/********************************************************************/
120	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::setPortOutput_M8(uint8_t portID_, uint8_t comSettings_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
121	  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;
122	  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_PRT;
123	  commandPacket.payloadLength = 1;
124	  commandPacket.payload[0] = portID_;
125	  commandPacket.validPacket = true;
126	  if(sendCommandPacket(true, true, maxWait_)) {

130	    return sendCommandPacket(false, true, maxWait_);

160	void TeenyUbloxConnect::setSerialRate(uint32_t baudrate_, uint8_t uartPort_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
161	  if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M8_MODULE) {
162	    setSerialRate_M8(baudrate_, uartPort_, maxWait_);
163	  } else if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M9_MODULE) {
164	    setSerialRate_M10(baudrate_, uartPort_, maxWait_);
165	  } else if(ubloxModuleType == UBLOX_M10_MODULE) {
166	    setSerialRate_M10(baudrate_, uartPort_, maxWait_);
167	  }
168	}
169	/********************************************************************/
170	void TeenyUbloxConnect::setSerialRate_M8(uint32_t baudrate_, uint8_t uartPort_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
171	  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;
172	  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_PRT;
173	  commandPacket.payloadLength = 1;
174	  commandPacket.payload[0] = uartPort_;
175	  commandPacket.validPacket = true;
176	  if(sendCommandPacket(true, true, maxWait_)) {
177	    commandPacket = responsePacket;
178	    for(uint8_t i = 0; i < 4; i++)
179	      commandPacket.payload[8 + i] = baudrate_ >> (8 * i);
180	    commandPacket.validPacket = true;
181	    sendCommandPacket(false, false, maxWait_); // ACK lost due to baudrate change
182	  }
183	}
184	/********************************************************************/
185	void TeenyUbloxConnect::setSerialRate_M10(uint32_t baudrate_, uint8_t uartPort_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
186	  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;
187	  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_VALSET;
188	  commandPacket.payloadLength = 12;
189	  commandPacket.payload[0] = 0;
190	  commandPacket.payload[1] = VALSET_LAYER_RAM;
191	  commandPacket.payload[2] = 0;
192	  commandPacket.payload[3] = 0;
193	  if(uartPort_ == COM_PORT_UART1) {
194	    for(uint8_t i = 0; i < 4; i++)
195	      commandPacket.payload[4 + i] = UBLOX_CFG_UART1_BAUDRATE >> (8 * i);
196	    for(uint8_t i = 0; i < 4; i++)
197	      commandPacket.payload[8 + i] = baudrate_ >> (8 * i);
198	    commandPacket.validPacket = true;
199	    sendCommandPacket(false, false, maxWait_); // ACK lost due to baudrate change
200	  }
201	}

203	/********************************************************************/
204	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::factoryReset() {
205	  if(clearConfiguration(0x0000FFFF)) {
206	    // Will need system reboot to access GPS module
207	    hardwareReset();
208	    return true;
209	  }
210	  return false;
211	}

214	void TeenyUbloxConnect::hardwareReset() {
215	  resetNAVSTATUSInfo(); // reset spoofing flags
216	  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;
217	  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_RST;
218	  commandPacket.payloadLength = UBX_CFG_RST_PAYLOADLENGTH;
219	  memcpy(commandPacket.payload, UBX_CFG_RST_HARDWARERESET_PAYLOAD, UBX_CFG_RST_PAYLOADLENGTH);
220	  commandPacket.validPacket = true;
221	  sendCommandPacket(false, false, 0);
222	}

> AGENT

Now I understand the problem clearly. The `TeenyUbloxConnect::begin()` function only accepts these exact protocol versions:
- 18.00 (M8), 32.01 (M9), 34.10 (M10)

The **UBX-G7020-KT is a u-blox 7**, which reports protocol version **14.xx** — not recognized, so `begin()` returns `false`. Everything downstream fails because `ubloxModuleType` stays `UBLOX_UNKNOWN_MODULE`.

Two fixes needed:
1. **Library**: Accept u-blox 7 protocol versions as M8-compatible (same UBX command set)
2. **`gps_connector.cpp`**: Use `UBX-CFG-NAV5` for dynamic model config on u-blox 7 (not CFG-VALSET which is M9+ only)

Both `.pio/libdeps` copies need updating (core2 and coreS3 builds):

546	bool GpsConnector::setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model, bool saveToFlash) {
547	  // Implementation based on u-blox M10 interface description (UBX-21035062)
548	  bool success = false;
549	  
550	  // Log the changes
551	  Serial.printf("Setting GPS dynamic model to %d (saveToFlash: %d)\n", model, saveToFlash);
552	  M5_LOGD("GNSS: Setting dynamic model to %d (saveToFlash: %d)", model, saveToFlash);
553	  
554	  // Create UBX-CFG-VALSET message payload
555	  uint8_t msgPayload[12]; // UBX-CFG-VALSET message payload
556	  
557	  // Header: version, layer, reserved
558	  msgPayload[0] = 0x00; // version 0
559	  msgPayload[1] = saveToFlash ? UBX_CFG_LAYER_ALL : UBX_CFG_LAYER_RAM; // layers: RAM only or RAM+BBR+Flash
560	  msgPayload[2] = 0x00; // reserved
561	  msgPayload[3] = 0x00; // reserved
562	  
563	  // Key: CFG-NAVSPG-DYNMODEL (0x20110021)
564	  msgPayload[4] = 0x21; // LSB
565	  msgPayload[5] = 0x00;
566	  msgPayload[6] = 0x11;
567	  msgPayload[7] = 0x20; // MSB
568	  
569	  // Value: dynamic model (1 byte, padded to 4 bytes)
570	  msgPayload[8] = (uint8_t)model; // Dynamic model value
571	  msgPayload[9] = 0x00; // padding
572	  msgPayload[10] = 0x00; // padding
573	  msgPayload[11] = 0x00; // padding
574	  
575	  // Send the UBX-CFG-VALSET message directly using our custom method
576	  if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_CFG_VALSET, msgPayload, sizeof(msgPayload))) {
577	    // Wait a bit for the message to be processed
578	    delay(100);
579	    
580	    // Store the current model
581	    _current_model = model;
582	    success = true;
583	    
584	    // Log the model that was set
585	    const char* modelName = "UNKNOWN";
586	    switch(model) {
587	      case DYNMODEL_PORT: modelName = "PORTABLE"; break;
588	      case DYNMODEL_STATIONARY: modelName = "STATIONARY"; break;
589	      case DYNMODEL_PEDESTRIAN: modelName = "PEDESTRIAN"; break;
590	      case DYNMODEL_AUTOMOTIVE: modelName = "AUTOMOTIVE"; break;
591	      case DYNMODEL_SEA: modelName = "SEA"; break;
592	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_1G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 1G"; break;
593	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_2G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 2G"; break;
594	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_4G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 4G"; break;
595	      case DYNMODEL_WRIST: modelName = "WRIST"; break;
596	    }
597	    
598	    Serial.printf("GPS set to %s mode\n", modelName);
599	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Set to %s mode (saveToFlash: %d)", modelName, saveToFlash);
600	    
601	    // Save to NVS for persistence across power cycles
602	    // Note: We need access to LocalStorage instance to save this
603	    // This will be handled by the calling code that has access to LocalStorage
604	    
605	  } else {
606	    Serial.println("Failed to send dynamic model change command");
607	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model change command");
608	  }
609	  
610	  return success;
611	}

break;\n      case DYNMODEL_WRIST: modelName = \"WRIST\"; break;\n    }\n    \n    Serial.printf(\"GPS set to %s mode\\n\", modelName);\n    M5_LOGD(\"GNSS: Set to %s mode (saveToFlash: %d)\", modelName, saveToFlash);\n    \n    // Save to NVS for persistence across power cycles\n    // Note: We need access to LocalStorage instance to save this\n    // This will be handled by the calling code that has access to LocalStorage\n    \n  } else {\n    Serial.println(\"Failed to send dynamic model change command\");\n    M5_LOGD(\"GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model change command\");\n  }\n  \n  return success;\n}",
  "new_string": "bool GpsConnector::setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model, bool saveToFlash) {\n  bool success = false;\n  M5_LOGD(\"GNSS: Setting dynamic model to %d (saveToFlash: %d)\", model, saveToFlash);\n\n  if (data.protocolVersionHigh < 18) {\n    // u-blox 7 (e.g. UBX-G7020-KT): use UBX-CFG-NAV5 (0x06/0x24)\n    // CFG-VALSET is not available on protocol versions below 18\n    uint8_t nav5Payload[36] = {0};\n    nav5Payload[0] = 0x01; // mask LSB: apply dynModel only\n    nav5Payload[1] = 0x00; // mask MSB\n    nav5Payload[2] = (uint8_t)model; // dynModel\n    nav5Payload[3] = 0x03; // fixMode: auto 2D/3D\n    if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_ID_CFG_NAV5, nav5Payload, sizeof(nav5Payload))) {\n      delay(100);\n      _current_model = model;\n      success = true;\n    }\n  } else {\n    // u-blox 8+ (M8/M9/M10): use UBX-CFG-VALSET (0x06/0x8A)\n    uint8_t msgPayload[12] = {0};\n    msgPayload[0] = 0x00; // version 0\n    msgPayload[1] = saveToFlash ? UBX_CFG_LAYER_ALL : UBX_CFG_LAYER_RAM;\n    // Key: CFG-NAVSPG-DYNMODEL (0x20110021)\n    msgPayload[4] = 0x21;\n    msgPayload[5] = 0x00;\n    msgPayload[6] = 0x11;\n    msgPayload[7] = 0x20;\n    msgPayload[8] = (uint8_t)model;\n    if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_CFG_VALSET, msgPayload, sizeof(msgPayload))) {\n      delay(100);\n      _current_model = model;\n      success = true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (success) {\n    const char* modelName = \"UNKNOWN\";\n    switch(model) {\n      case DYNMODEL_PORT: modelName = \"PORTABLE\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_STATIONARY: modelName = \"STATIONARY\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_PEDESTRIAN: modelName = \"PEDESTRIAN\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_AUTOMOTIVE: modelName = \"AUTOMOTIVE\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_SEA: modelName = \"SEA\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_1G: modelName = \"AIRBORNE 1G\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_2G: modelName = \"AIRBORNE 2G\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_4G: modelName = \"AIRBORNE 4G\"; break;\n      case DYNMODEL_WRIST: modelName = \"WRIST\"; break;\n    }\n    M5_LOGD(\"GNSS: Set to %s mode (saveToFlash: %d)\", modelName, saveToFlash);\n  } else {\n    M5_LOGD(\"GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model change command\");\n  }\n\n  return success;\n}"

Good. There's one issue with `setDynamicModel` — when `data.protocolVersionHigh == 0` (uninitialized), the check `< 18` would incorrectly use the NAV5 path. Let me check the condition: `readDynamicModelFromGPS()` is called during `activate()` before `data.protocolVersionHigh` is set... actually no, looking at line 113-121 of the original:

```cpp
data.protocolVersionHigh = _gnss.getProtocolVersionHigh();
data.protocolVersionLow = _gnss.getProtocolVersionLow();
...
readDynamicModelFromGPS();
```

The version is set before `readDynamicModelFromGPS()` is called. And `setDynamicModel()` is only called from user code after activation. But `protocolVersionHigh == 0` case in `setDynamicModel` — if the GPS isn't activated, calling it would use NAV5 incorrectly. Let me tighten the condition slightly:

> AGENT

Good — the `else` branch (which covers protocol version 0 or ≥ 18) uses CFG-VALSET, which is the correct default. The changes are complete.

Here's a summary of what was changed and why:

---

**Root cause:** The `TeenyUbloxConnect` library's `begin()` function only accepts three exact protocol versions (18.00 for M8, 32.01 for M9, 34.10 for M10). The **UBX-G7020-KT** is a u-blox 7 chip that reports **protocol version 14.xx**, which was not recognized. `begin()` returned `false`, leaving `ubloxModuleType = UBLOX_UNKNOWN_MODULE`, and all subsequent library calls (`setAutoNAVPVT`, `setPortOutput`, `getNAVPVT`, etc.) silently returned `false` — the GPS produced no data.

**Changes made:**

1. **[TeenyUbloxConnect.cpp](bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.cpp)** (both `.pio/libdeps` copies — core2 and coreS3):
   - Added a fallback in `begin()`: protocol versions below 18 (u-blox 7 range: 14.x/15.x) are now recognized and treated as M8-compatible. The u-blox 7 uses the same `CFG-PRT`/`NAV-PVT` command set as M8, so this is correct.
   - Also relaxed the M8 check from `== 18.00` to `== 18.x` (any minor version).

2. **[gps_connector.cpp](bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.cpp)**:
   - `setDynamicModel()`: On protocol version < 18 (u-blox 7), now uses **UBX-CFG-NAV5** (0x06/0x24) instead of CFG-VALSET (which is M9+ only).
   - `readDynamicModelFromGPS()`: Same — polls CFG-NAV5 on u-blox 7, CFG-VALGET on M8+.
   - `sendUBXMessage()`: Guarded `nullptr` payload to safely handle poll requests with zero-length payload.

> **Note:** The `.pio/libdeps/` library files may be overwritten if you run `pio lib update`. If that happens, re-apply the change to `TeenyUbloxConnect.cpp` in both target folders, or copy the library into the project's `lib/` directory to make the patch permanent.

1511	bool TeenyUbloxConnect::sendCommandPacket(bool expectResp_, bool expectAck_, uint16_t maxWait_) {
1512	  if(!commandPacket.validPacket) return false;
1513	  calcCommandPacketChecksum();
1514	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.synch1);
1515	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.synch2);
1516	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.messageClass);
1517	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.messageID);
1518	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.payloadLength & 0xFF);
1519	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.payloadLength >> 8);
1520	  //for(uint16_t i = 0; i < commandPacket.payloadLength; i++) {
1521	  //  serialPort->write(commandPacket.payload[i]);
1522	  //}
1523	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.payload, commandPacket.payloadLength);
1524	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.checksumA);
1525	  serialPort->write(commandPacket.checksumB);
1526	  commandPacket.validPacket = false;
1527	  if(!(expectResp_ || expectAck_)) {
1528	    return true;
1529	  }
1530	  processingUbloxCommand = true;
1531	  responsePacket.validPacket = false;
1532	  acknowledgePacket.validPacket = false;
1533	  uint32_t startTime = millis();
1534	  while((millis() - startTime) < maxWait_) {
1535	    checkUbloxInternal();
1536	    processIncomingPacket(commandPacket.messageClass, commandPacket.messageID);
1537	    if(expectResp_ && expectAck_ && responsePacket.validPacket && acknowledgePacket.validPacket) {
1538	      processingUbloxCommand = false;
1539	      return acknowledgePacket.messageID;
1540	    } else if(expectResp_ && (!expectAck_) && responsePacket.validPacket) {
1541	      processingUbloxCommand = false;
1542	      return true;
1543	    } else if((!expectResp_) && expectAck_ && acknowledgePacket.validPacket) {
1544	      processingUbloxCommand = false;
1545	      return acknowledgePacket.messageID;
1546	    }
1547	  }
1548	  processingUbloxCommand = false;
1549	  return false;
1550	}

1557	// Given a message, calc and store the two byte "8-Bit Fletcher"
1558	// checksum over the entirety of the message
1559	// This is called before we send a command packet
1560	void TeenyUbloxConnect::calcCommandPacketChecksum() {
1561	  commandPacket.checksumA = 0;
1562	  commandPacket.checksumB = 0;
1563	
1564	  commandPacket.checksumA += commandPacket.messageClass;
1565	  commandPacket.checksumB += commandPacket.checksumA;
1566	
1567	  commandPacket.checksumA += commandPacket.messageID;
1568	  commandPacket.checksumB += commandPacket.checksumA;
1569	
1570	  commandPacket.checksumA += (commandPacket.payloadLength & 0xFF);
1571	  commandPacket.checksumB += commandPacket.checksumA;
1572	
1573	  commandPacket.checksumA += (commandPacket.payloadLength >> 8);
1574	  commandPacket.checksumB += commandPacket.checksumA;
1575	
1576	  for(uint16_t i = 0; i < commandPacket.payloadLength; i++) {
1577	    commandPacket.checksumA += commandPacket.payload[i];
1578	    commandPacket.checksumB += commandPacket.checksumA;
1579	  }
1580	}

> AGENT

The GPS **is working** — it has a fix and is sending NMEA at 9600 baud. The raw dump shows:
```
$GPRMC,073148.00,A,3428.96322,N,13609.79138,E,0.411,,300326,,,A*
```

The problem is the UBX handshake is failing. The library sends UBX-CFG-PRT and expects both a **response + ACK**, but the GPS may not be receiving our TX commands (one-way wiring), or u-blox 7 doesn't ACK poll requests.

Let me add a TX test to the diagnostic — send a raw UBX command and see if the GPS responds:

> TOOL

> AGENT

Yes, the GPS data is perfect at 9600 baud — valid $GPRMC with a fix in Japan. The GPS hardware is fine. The problem is the UBX handshake: the library sends CFG-PRT and requires **both** a response **and** an ACK. The u-blox 7 may not ACK poll requests, or the TX line might be one-way.

Let me update the diagnostic to test TX, and fix the library to also try without ACK requirement:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01XtrNy3odyHMfjcdKFPRcJL
```json

  "new_string": "  else if (!_raw_dump_done && _tried_4800_at > 0 && (millis() - _tried_4800_at > 1200)) {\n    // All UBX baud-rate attempts failed — test TX line and check for UBX response\n    _raw_dump_done = true;\n    _serial_conn.updateBaudRate(9600);\n    while (_serial_conn.available()) _serial_conn.read(); // flush RX\n    // Send a raw UBX-CFG-PRT poll (class 0x06, id 0x06, payload = UART1 port id 0x01)\n    // B5 62 06 06 01 00 01 0E 24\n    const uint8_t ubxPoll[] = {0xB5, 0x62, 0x06, 0x06, 0x01, 0x00, 0x01, 0x0E, 0x24};\n    _serial_conn.write(ubxPoll, sizeof(ubxPoll));\n    _serial_conn.flush();\n    delay(300); // wait for GPS to respond\n    uint8_t buf[128];\n    int n = 0;\n    uint32_t t = millis();\n    while (n < (int)sizeof(buf) && (millis() - t) < 300) {\n      if (_serial_conn.available()) buf[n++] = _serial_conn.read();\n    }\n    if (n > 0) {\n      bool hasUbx = false;\n      for (int i = 0; i < n - 1; i++) {\n        if (buf[i] == 0xB5 && buf[i+1] == 0x62) { hasUbx = true; break; }\n      }\n      char hex[3 * 32 + 1] = {0};\n      int show = n < 32 ? n : 32;\n      for (int i = 0; i < show; i++) snprintf(hex + i * 3, 4, \"%02X \", buf[i]);\n      M5_LOGI(\"GNSS: TX test: %d bytes back, UBX=%s, hex: %s\", n, hasUbx ? \"YES\" : \"NO\", hex);\n      if (!hasUbx) {\n        M5_LOGW(\"GNSS: No UBX response — GPS TX→RX(GPIO18) OK, but GPS RX←TX(GPIO17) may be unconnected\");\n      }\n    } else {\n      M5_LOGW(\"GNSS: No bytes at 9600 — check wiring RX=GPIO18, TX=GPIO17 on CoreS3\");\n    }\n    return false;\n  }"

> AGENT

The simplest fix: change `pollUART1Port_M8` to only require a **response** (not ACK) — u-blox 7 may not ACK poll requests, but will still send the response data. M8 works fine with this too:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01Gc8ndb41wfL2xNT6sqWn33
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/bGeigieZen/.pio/libdeps/m5stack-cores3-unified/TeenyUbloxConnect/src/TeenyUbloxConnect.cpp",
  "old_string": "bool TeenyUbloxConnect::pollUART1Port_M8(uint16_t maxWait_, bool flushPort_) {\n  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;\n  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_PRT;\n  commandPacket.payloadLength = 1;\n  commandPacket.payload[0] = COM_PORT_UART1;\n  commandPacket.validPacket = true;\n  if(flushPort_) while(serialPort->available()) serialPort->read();\n  return sendCommandPacket(true, true, maxWait_);\n}",
  "new_string": "bool TeenyUbloxConnect::pollUART1Port_M8(uint16_t maxWait_, bool flushPort_) {\n  commandPacket.messageClass = UBX_CLASS_CFG;\n  commandPacket.messageID = UBX_CFG_PRT;\n  commandPacket.payloadLength = 1;\n  commandPacket.payload[0] = COM_PORT_UART1;\n  commandPacket.validPacket = true;\n  if(flushPort_) while(serialPort->available()) serialPort->read();\n  // Use response-only (no ACK required): u-blox 7 does not ACK poll requests\n  return sendCommandPacket(true, false, maxWait_);\n}"

> AGENT

The TX test is definitive: **GPIO17 (TX) is not reaching the GPS RX pin** — the connection is one-way. The GPS outputs NMEA fine but can't receive our UBX commands.

The solution is to implement NMEA parsing as a fallback. Let me check if TinyGPS++ is already a dependency, and if not, add NMEA parsing directly:

{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/bGeigieZen/bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.cpp",
  "old_string": "bool GpsConnector::restoreGpsMemoryFromNVS() {\n  M5_LOGI(\"GPS: Checking for saved memory data in internal storage\");\n  \n  // The U-blox M10 automatically restores from its internal backup memory\n  // when it powers up, so we don't need to actively restore data\n  // The backup memory contains almanac, ephemeris, and satellite data\n  \n  M5_LOGI(\"GPS: Memory restore relies on U-blox internal backup memory\");\n  \n  return true;\n}",
  "new_string": "bool GpsConnector::restoreGpsMemoryFromNVS() {\n  M5_LOGI(\"GPS: Checking for saved memory data in internal storage\");\n  M5_LOGI(\"GPS: Memory restore relies on U-blox internal backup memory\");\n  return true;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// NMEA fallback mode — used when GPS TX is connected but GPS RX is not,\n// so only one-way communication (receive NMEA) is possible.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ndouble GpsConnector::nmeaCoordToDecimal(const char* coord, char direction) {\n  double val = atof(coord);\n  int deg = (int)(val / 100.0);\n  double min = val - deg * 100.0;\n  double result = deg + min / 60.0;\n  if (direction == 'S' || direction == 'W') result = -result;\n  return result;\n}\n\nbool GpsConnector::parseNmeaSentence(const char* sentence) {\n  // Validate checksum (XOR of bytes between '$' and '*')\n  const char* star = strchr(sentence, '*');\n  if (star != nullptr && strlen(star) >= 3) {\n    uint8_t calc = 0;\n    for (const char* p = sentence + 1; p < star; p++) calc ^= (uint8_t)*p;\n    uint8_t recv = (uint8_t)strtol(star + 1, nullptr, 16);\n    if (calc != recv) return false;\n  }\n\n  bool isRmc = (strncmp(sentence, \"$GPRMC\", 6) == 0 || strncmp(sentence, \"$GNRMC\", 6) == 0);\n  bool isGga = (strncmp(sentence, \"$GPGGA\", 6) == 0 || strncmp(sentence, \"$GNGGA\", 6) == 0);\n  if (!isRmc && !isGga) return false;\n\n  // Copy and strip checksum for field splitting\n  char buf[128];\n  strncpy(buf, sentence, sizeof(buf) - 1);\n  buf[sizeof(buf) - 1] = '\\0';\n  char* s = strchr(buf, '*');\n  if (s) *s = '\\0';\n\n  // Split on commas\n  char* fields[15] = {nullptr};\n  int nf = 0;\n  char* p = buf;\n  while (nf < 15) {\n    fields[nf++] = p;\n    p = strchr(p, ',');\n    if (!p) break;\n    *p++ = '\\0';\n  }\n\n  if (isRmc && nf >= 10) {\n    // $G?RMC,HHMMSS.ss,A,DDMM.mmm,N,DDDMM.mmm,E,speed,course,DDMMYY,...\n    const char* tstr = fields[1];\n    const char* stat = fields[2];\n    const char* lat  = fields[3];\n    const char* ns   = fields[4];\n    const char* lon  = fields[5];\n    const char* ew   = fields[6];\n    const char* spd  = fields[7];\n    const char* crs  = fields[8];\n    const char* dstr = fields[9];\n\n    if (strlen(tstr) >= 6) {\n      char tmp[3] = {tstr[0], tstr[1], '\\0'}; data.hour   = atoi(tmp);\n      tmp[0] = tstr[2]; tmp[1] = tstr[3];     data.minute = atoi(tmp);\n      tmp[0] = tstr[4]; tmp[1] = tstr[5];     data.second = atoi(tmp);\n      data.time_valid = true;\n      time_timer.restart();\n    }\n    if (strlen(dstr) >= 6) {\n      char tmp[3] = {dstr[0], dstr[1], '\\0'}; data.day   = atoi(tmp);\n      tmp[0] = dstr[2]; tmp[1] = dstr[3];     data.month = atoi(tmp);\n      tmp[0] = dstr[4]; tmp[1] = dstr[5];     data.year  = 2000 + atoi(tmp);\n      data.date_valid = true;\n      date_timer.restart();\n    }\n    if (stat[0] == 'A' && strlen(lat) > 0 && strlen(lon) > 0) {\n      _last_latitude  = data.latitude;\n      _last_longitude = data.longitude;\n      data.latitude  = nmeaCoordToDecimal(lat, ns[0]);\n      data.longitude = nmeaCoordToDecimal(lon, ew[0]);\n      data.gSpeed    = (int32_t)(atof(spd) * 514.444); // knots → mm/s\n      data.heading_degree = strlen(crs) > 0 ? atof(crs) : 0.0;\n      const auto dist = haversine_km(data.latitude, data.longitude, _last_latitude, _last_longitude);\n      data.location_valid = (dist < 0.5);\n      location_timer.restart();\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (isGga && nf >= 10) {\n    // $G?GGA,time,lat,N,lon,E,quality,nsats,hdop,alt,M,...\n    const char* qual  = fields[6];\n    const char* nsats = fields[7];\n    const char* hdop  = fields[8];\n    const char* alt   = fields[9];\n    if (qual[0] != '0' && strlen(nsats) > 0) {\n      data.satsInView = atoi(nsats);\n      data.numSV      = data.satsInView;\n      data.pdop       = strlen(hdop) > 0 ? atof(hdop) : 0.0;\n      data.altitudeMSL = strlen(alt) > 0 ? atof(alt) : 0.0;\n      return true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return false;\n}\n\nint8_t GpsConnector::produceDataNmea() {\n  auto ret_status = e_worker_idle;\n\n  while (_serial_conn.available()) {\n    char c = (char)_serial_conn.read();\n    if (c == '$') {\n      _nmea_len = 0;\n      _nmea_buf[_nmea_len++] = c;\n    } else if (c == '\\n' || c == '\\r') {\n      if (_nmea_len > 6) {\n        _nmea_buf[_nmea_len] = '\\0';\n        if (parseNmeaSentence(_nmea_buf)) {\n          ret_status = e_worker_data_read;\n        }\n      }\n      _nmea_len = 0;\n    } else if (_nmea_len > 0 && _nmea_len < (uint8_t)(sizeof(_nmea_buf) - 1)) {\n      _nmea_buf[_nmea_len++] = c;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (location_timer.isExpired()) data.location_valid = false;\n  if (time_timer.isExpired()) {\n    data.time_valid = false;\n    data.hour = GPS_INVALID_HOUR;\n    data.minute = GPS_INVALID_MINUTE;\n    data.second = GPS_INVALID_SECOND;\n  }\n  if (date_timer.isExpired()) {\n    data.date_valid = false;\n    data.year  = GPS_INVALID_YEAR;\n    data.month = GPS_INVALID_MONTH;\n    data.day   = GPS_INVALID_DAY;\n  }\n\n  ApiDataCache::instance().update(data);\n  return ret_status;\n}"
}

> AGENT

The display shows no GPS data — the NMEA mode activated but the parsed data isn't showing. The most likely cause is the **first-fix distance check**: on the very first $GPRMC, `_last_latitude = 0`, so `haversine_km(34.48°N, 136°E, 0, 0)` ≈ 15,000 km → `location_valid = false`. The second sentence (1s later) should fix it, but let me also add a bypass for the first fix and build:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01HehmGyxjfkQPUYhoriFKnr
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/bGeigieZen/bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.cpp",
  "old_string": "    if (stat[0] == 'A' && strlen(lat) > 0 && strlen(lon) > 0) {\n      _last_latitude  = data.latitude;\n      _last_longitude = data.longitude;\n      data.latitude  = nmeaCoordToDecimal(lat, ns[0]);\n      data.longitude = nmeaCoordToDecimal(lon, ew[0]);\n      data.gSpeed    = (int32_t)(atof(spd) * 514.444); // knots → mm/s\n      data.heading_degree = strlen(crs) > 0 ? atof(crs) : 0.0;\n      const auto dist = haversine_km(data.latitude, data.longitude, _last_latitude, _last_longitude);\n      data.location_valid = (dist < 0.5);\n      location_timer.restart();\n      return true;\n    }",
  "new_string": "    if (stat[0] == 'A' && strlen(lat) > 0 && strlen(lon) > 0) {\n      double new_lat = nmeaCoordToDecimal(lat, ns[0]);\n      double new_lon = nmeaCoordToDecimal(lon, ew[0]);\n      // Skip distance sanity check on first fix (last position is 0,0)\n      bool first_fix = (_last_latitude == 0.0 && _last_longitude == 0.0);\n      const auto dist = haversine_km(new_lat, new_lon, _last_latitude, _last_longitude);\n      _last_latitude  = data.latitude;\n      _last_longitude = data.longitude;\n      data.latitude  = new_lat;\n      data.longitude = new_lon;\n      data.gSpeed    = (int32_t)(atof(spd) * 514.444); // knots → mm/s\n      data.heading_degree = strlen(crs) > 0 ? atof(crs) : 0.0;\n      data.location_valid = first_fix || (dist < 0.5);\n      location_timer.restart();\n      return true;\n    }"
}

```
   * Data read = 0, considered fresh produced data to be handled
   * Anything above 0 can be used for custom errors
   */
  typedef enum Status {
    e_worker_processing = -2, // For async
    e_worker_idle = -1,
    e_worker_data_read = 0,
    e_worker_error,
  } Status;

  explicit BaseWorker(uint32_t break_duration = 0);
  virtual ~BaseWorker() = default;

  explicit BaseWorker(BaseWorker& copy) = delete;


  /**
   * Get status
   * @return
   */
  int8_t get_status() const;

  /**
   * Get status
   * @return
   */
  uint32_t get_last_produce() const;

  /**
   * Checks if fresh data is read
   * @return
--
  virtual int8_t produce_data() = 0;

  /**
   * The main function to implement in sub classes, store produced work in `data` property
   * Used by process workers
   * @return status code (BaseWorker::Status or any custom)
   */
  virtual int8_t produce_data(const worker_map_t& workers) = 0;

  /**
   * Function that is run async in a separate task, do NOT access worker.data from this
   * prepare data inside the instance, to be copied afterwards in the main loop by implementing `finish_produced_data`
   * @return status code (BaseWorker::Status or any custom)
   */
  virtual int8_t produce_async_data();

  /**
   * Implement this function to finalize async results to the data to be used in the rest of the system.
   * This function is called by the main thread after the task to produce async data was finished.
   */
  virtual void finish_produced_data();

  virtual bool is_process_worker() const = 0;

  int8_t start_task(const char* task_name, uint32_t memory=1024, uint8_t priority=5, uint8_t core=0);
  void kill_task();

  virtual bool task_running() const;
```

> AGENT

The sentences are garbled — `$3,023034,G14L.87` is not valid NMEA. The UART is in a degraded state after multiple `updateBaudRate` calls. The fix is to do a full `begin()` reinit when entering NMEA mode:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01LWfMzW1AfshCHiKpL4HQ7B
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/bGeigieZen/bgeigiezen_firmware/workers/gps_connector.cpp",
  "old_string": "      if (hasNmea && !hasUbx) {\n        M5_LOGI(\"GNSS: TX unconnected — switching to NMEA fallback mode (RX-only)\");\n        _nmea_mode = true;\n        memset(_nmea_buf, 0, sizeof(_nmea_buf));\n        _nmea_len = 0;\n        return true;  // GPS is usable via NMEA\n      }",
  "new_string": "      if (hasNmea && !hasUbx) {\n        M5_LOGI(\"GNSS: TX unconnected — switching to NMEA fallback mode (RX-only)\");\n        // Fully reinitialise the UART to clear any state left by updateBaudRate() calls\n        _serial_conn.end();\n        delay(50);\n#ifdef CONFIG_IDF_TARGET_ESP32S3\n        _serial_conn.begin(9600, SERIAL_8N1, RXD2, TXD2);\n#else\n        _serial_conn.begin(9600);\n#endif\n        delay(100);\n        while (_serial_conn.available()) _serial_conn.read(); // flush any stale bytes\n        _nmea_mode = true;\n        memset(_nmea_buf, 0, sizeof(_nmea_buf));\n        _nmea_len = 0;\n        return true;\n      }"
}

208	// Simple warm seed format
209	// magic 0x47574D53 ('GWMS'), version 1, lat/lon in 1e-7 deg, alt mm, date/time
210	struct WarmSeedBinV1 {
211	  uint32_t magic;     // 'GWMS'

236	bool GpsConnector::saveWarmStartSeedToSD() {
237	  // Ensure we have valid data
238	  if (!(data.location_valid && data.date_valid && data.time_valid)) {
239	    M5_LOGW("GPS: Cannot save warm seed — invalid or incomplete GNSS data");
240	    return false;
241	  }
242	
243	  // Ensure directory exists
244	  if (!SD.exists(kSeedDir)) {
245	    if (!SD.mkdir(kSeedDir)) {
246	      M5_LOGE("GPS: Failed to create %s directory on SD", kSeedDir);
247	      return false;
248	    }
249	  }
250	
251	  WarmSeedBinV1 seed{};
252	  seed.magic = kSeedMagic;
253	  seed.version = kSeedVer;
254	  seed.lat_e7 = static_cast<int32_t>(data.latitude * 1e7);
255	  seed.lon_e7 = static_cast<int32_t>(data.longitude * 1e7);
256	  seed.alt_mm = static_cast<int32_t>(data.altitudeMSL * 1000.0);
257	  seed.year = data.year;
258	  seed.month = data.month;
259	  seed.day = data.day;
260	  seed.hour = data.hour;
261	  seed.minute = data.minute;
262	  seed.second = data.second;
263	
264	  File f = SD.open(kSeedPath, FILE_WRITE);
265	  if (!f) {
266	    M5_LOGE("GPS: Failed to open %s for write", kSeedPath);
267	    return false;
268	  }
269	  size_t written = f.write(reinterpret_cast<const uint8_t*>(&seed), sizeof(seed));
270	  f.flush();
271	  f.close();
272	  if (written != sizeof(seed)) {
273	    M5_LOGE("GPS: Failed to write full seed (wrote %u/%u bytes)", (unsigned)written, (unsigned)sizeof(seed));
274	    return false;
275	  }
276	  M5_LOGI("GPS: Warm-start seed saved to %s", kSeedPath);
277	  return true;
278	}
279	
280	bool GpsConnector::injectWarmStartSeedFromSD() {
281	  if (!SD.exists(kSeedPath)) {
282	    M5_LOGI("GPS: No warm-start seed found at %s", kSeedPath);
283	    return false;
284	  }
285	
286	  File f = SD.open(kSeedPath, FILE_READ);
287	  if (!f) {
288	    M5_LOGE("GPS: Failed to open %s for read", kSeedPath);
289	    return false;
290	  }
291	
292	  // Check file age
293	  time_t fileTime = f.getLastWrite();
294	  time_t now = time(nullptr);
295	  if (now > 0 && fileTime > 0 && (now - fileTime) > kSeedMaxAge) {
296	    f.close();
297	    M5_LOGW("GPS: Warm seed too old (%u seconds), skipping injection", (unsigned)(now - fileTime));
298	    return false;
299	  }

551	  while (f.available()) {
552	    totalRead++;
553	    int bi = f.read();
554	    if (bi < 0) break;
555	    b = (uint8_t)bi;
556	    switch (state) {
557	      case FIND_SYNC1:
558	        if (b == 0xB5) { fidx = 0; frame[fidx++] = b; state = FIND_SYNC2; }
559	        break;
560	      case FIND_SYNC2:
561	        if (b == 0x62) { frame[fidx++] = b; state = READ_HEADER; hidx = 0; }
562	        else { state = FIND_SYNC1; fidx = 0; }
563	        break;
564	      case READ_HEADER:
565	        header[hidx++] = b; frame[fidx++] = b;
566	        if (hidx == 4) {
567	          ck_a = header[0]; ck_b = ck_a; // class
568	          ck_a += header[1]; ck_b += ck_a; // id
569	          ck_a += header[2]; ck_b += ck_a; // len LSB
570	          ck_a += header[3]; ck_b += ck_a; // len MSB
571	          len = (uint16_t)header[2] | ((uint16_t)header[3] << 8);
572	          pleft = len;
573	          state = (len == 0) ? READ_CKS : READ_PAYLOAD;
574	        }
575	        break;
576	      case READ_PAYLOAD:
577	        frame[fidx++] = b;
578	        ck_a += b; ck_b += ck_a;
579	        if (--pleft == 0) { state = READ_CKS; }
580	        break;
581	      case READ_CKS:
582	        frame[fidx++] = b;
583	        if (fidx == 6 + len + 1) { // just read CK_A
584	          if (b != ck_a) { state = FIND_SYNC1; fidx = 0; break; }
585	        } else if (fidx == 6 + len + 2) { // just read CK_B
586	          if (b != ck_b) { state = FIND_SYNC1; fidx = 0; break; }
587	          // Valid UBX frame complete; send and wait for ACK if it's DBD
588	          uint8_t cls = header[0];
589	          uint8_t id  = header[1];
590	          if (cls == 0x13 && id == 0x80) {
591	            _serial_conn.write(frame, fidx);
592	            _serial_conn.flush();
593	            bytes += fidx; frames++;
594	            // Minimal pacing - don't wait for ACKs during boot
595	            delay(1);
596	          }
597	          // Reset for next frame
598	          state = FIND_SYNC1; fidx = 0;
599	        }
600	        break;
601	    }
602	  }
603	  f.close();
604	  M5_LOGI("GPS: Database restore sent: %u frames, %u bytes (read %u/%u bytes from file)", 
605	          (unsigned)frames, (unsigned)bytes, (unsigned)totalRead, (unsigned)fileSize);
606	  return frames > 0;

609	/**
610	 * Set the GPS dynamic platform model
611	 * @param model The dynamic model to set (e.g., DYNMODEL_PORT, DYNMODEL_AIR4)
612	 * @param saveToFlash If true, saves the setting to flash memory so it persists after power cycles
613	 * @return true if successful, false otherwise
614	 */
615	bool GpsConnector::setDynamicModel(UbxDynamicModel model, bool saveToFlash) {
616	  bool success = false;
617	  M5_LOGD("GNSS: Setting dynamic model to %d (saveToFlash: %d)", model, saveToFlash);
618	
619	  if (data.protocolVersionHigh > 0 && data.protocolVersionHigh < 18) {
620	    // u-blox 7 (e.g. UBX-G7020-KT): use UBX-CFG-NAV5 (0x06/0x24)
621	    // CFG-VALSET is not available on protocol versions below 18
622	    uint8_t nav5Payload[36] = {0};
623	    nav5Payload[0] = 0x01; // mask LSB: apply dynModel only
624	    nav5Payload[1] = 0x00; // mask MSB
625	    nav5Payload[2] = (uint8_t)model; // dynModel
626	    nav5Payload[3] = 0x03; // fixMode: auto 2D/3D
627	    if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_ID_CFG_NAV5, nav5Payload, sizeof(nav5Payload))) {
628	      delay(100);
629	      _current_model = model;
630	      success = true;
631	    }
632	  } else {
633	    // u-blox 8+ (M8/M9/M10): use UBX-CFG-VALSET (0x06/0x8A)
634	    uint8_t msgPayload[12] = {0};
635	    msgPayload[0] = 0x00; // version 0
636	    msgPayload[1] = saveToFlash ? UBX_CFG_LAYER_ALL : UBX_CFG_LAYER_RAM;
637	    // Key: CFG-NAVSPG-DYNMODEL (0x20110021)
638	    msgPayload[4] = 0x21;
639	    msgPayload[5] = 0x00;
640	    msgPayload[6] = 0x11;
641	    msgPayload[7] = 0x20;
642	    msgPayload[8] = (uint8_t)model;
643	    if (sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_CFG_VALSET, msgPayload, sizeof(msgPayload))) {
644	      delay(100);
645	      _current_model = model;
646	      success = true;
647	    }
648	  }
649	
650	  if (success) {
651	    const char* modelName = "UNKNOWN";
652	    switch(model) {
653	      case DYNMODEL_PORT: modelName = "PORTABLE"; break;
654	      case DYNMODEL_STATIONARY: modelName = "STATIONARY"; break;
655	      case DYNMODEL_PEDESTRIAN: modelName = "PEDESTRIAN"; break;
656	      case DYNMODEL_AUTOMOTIVE: modelName = "AUTOMOTIVE"; break;
657	      case DYNMODEL_SEA: modelName = "SEA"; break;
658	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_1G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 1G"; break;
659	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_2G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 2G"; break;
660	      case DYNMODEL_AIRBORNE_4G: modelName = "AIRBORNE 4G"; break;
661	      case DYNMODEL_WRIST: modelName = "WRIST"; break;
662	    }
663	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Set to %s mode (saveToFlash: %d)", modelName, saveToFlash);
664	  } else {
665	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model change command");
666	  }
667	
668	  return success;
669	}

690	/**
691	 * Read the current dynamic model directly from the GPS module
692	 * @return true if successful, false otherwise
693	 */
694	bool GpsConnector::readDynamicModelFromGPS() {
695	  bool success = false;
696	
697	  if (data.protocolVersionHigh > 0 && data.protocolVersionHigh < 18) {
698	    // u-blox 7: poll UBX-CFG-NAV5 (no payload = poll request)
699	    success = sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_ID_CFG_NAV5, nullptr, 0);
700	  } else {
701	    // u-blox 8+ (M8/M9/M10): poll UBX-CFG-VALGET
702	    uint8_t msgPayload[8] = {0};
703	    msgPayload[1] = UBX_CFG_LAYER_RAM;
704	    msgPayload[4] = 0x21; // Key: CFG-NAVSPG-DYNMODEL LSB
705	    msgPayload[5] = 0x00;
706	    msgPayload[6] = 0x11;
707	    msgPayload[7] = 0x20; // MSB
708	    success = sendUBXMessage(UBX_CLASS_CFG, UBX_CFG_VALGET, msgPayload, sizeof(msgPayload));
709	  }
710	
711	  if (success) {
712	    delay(100);
713	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Current dynamic model (stored): %d", _current_model);
714	  } else {
715	    M5_LOGD("GNSS: Failed to send dynamic model query command");
716	  }
717	
718	  return success;
719	}

722	int8_t GpsConnector::produce_data() {
723	  if (_nmea_mode) {
724	    return produceDataNmea();
725	  }
726	  auto ret_status = e_worker_idle;
727	
728	  // getPVT returns true if there is a fresh navigation solution available.
729	  // "LLH" is longitude, latitude, height.
730	  // getPVT() returns UTC date and time.
731	  // Do not use GNSS time, see u-blox spec section 9.
732	  if (_gnss.getNAVPVT()) {
733	    // M5_LOGD("[%d] _gnss.getPVT() is true.", millis());
734	
735	    data.satsInView = _gnss.getNumSV(); // Satellites In View (numSV)
736	
737	    if (_gnss.getFixType() == 2 || _gnss.getFixType() == 3) {
738	      // M5_LOGD("[%d] fix type is 2D or 3D.", millis());
739	      data.pdop = _gnss.getPDOP() * 1e-2; // Position Dilution of Precision
740	      _last_latitude = data.latitude;
741	      _last_longitude = data.longitude;
742	      data.latitude = _gnss.getLatitude() * 1e-7;
743	      data.longitude = _gnss.getLongitude() * 1e-7;
744	      data.heading_degree = _gnss.getHeading() * 1e-5;
745	      if (data.heading_degree >= NNW || data.heading_degree < NNE) {
746	        data.heading = GnssData::NORTH;
747	      }
748	      if (data.heading_degree >= NNE || data.heading_degree < ENE) {
749	        data.heading = GnssData::NORTHEAST;
750	      }
751	      if (data.heading_degree >= ENE || data.heading_degree < ESE) {
752	        data.heading = GnssData::EAST;
753	      }
754	      if (data.heading_degree >= ESE || data.heading_degree < SSE) {
755	        data.heading = GnssData::SOUTHEAST;
756	      }
757	      if (data.heading_degree >= SSE || data.heading_degree < SSW) {
758	        data.heading = GnssData::SOUTH;
759	      }
760	      if (data.heading_degree >= SSW || data.heading_degree < WSW) {
761	        data.heading = GnssData::SOUTHWEST;
762	      }
763	      if (data.heading_degree >= WSW || data.heading_degree < WNW) {
764	        data.heading = GnssData::WEST;
765	      }
766	      if (data.heading_degree >= WNW || data.heading_degree < NNW) {
767	        data.heading = GnssData::NORTHWEST;
768	      }
769	      data.altitudeMSL = _gnss.getFixType() == 3 ? _gnss.getAltitudeMSL() * 1e-3 : 0; // Above MSL (not ellipsoid)
770	      const auto distance_step = haversine_km(data.latitude, data.longitude, _last_latitude, _last_longitude);
771	
772	
773	      // Retrieve extra confidence indicators from NavPvt
774	      data.hAcc = _gnss.getHorizontalAccEst();  // mm Horizontal accuracy estimate for Long/Lat
775	      data.vAcc = _gnss.getVerticalAccEst();  // mm Vertical accuracy estimate for Long/Lat
776	      data.velN = _gnss.getVelN();  // mm/s NED north velocity
777	      data.velE = _gnss.getVelE();  // mm/s NED east velocity
778	      data.velD = _gnss.getVelD();  // mm/s NED down velocity
779	      data.gSpeed = _gnss.getGroundSpeed();  // Ground Speed (2-D)
780	      data.headMot = _gnss.getHeading();  // Heading of motion (2-D)
781	      data.sAcc = _gnss.getSpeedAccEst();
782	      data.headAcc = _gnss.getHeadingAccEst();
783	
784	      data.location_valid = _gnss.getGnssFixOk() && distance_step < 0.5; // Airplanes fly at 255m per second, 500 meter check is good enough
785	      location_timer.restart();
786	      ret_status = e_worker_data_read;
787	      // DEBUG: Compare PDOP from NAV-PVT and HDOP from NAV-DOP
788	      // M5_LOGD("[%d] GnssFixOk (type = %d)."
789	      //               "  SATS: %d; PDOP: %d; HDOP: %d\n",
790	      //               millis(), _gnss.getFixType(),
791	      //               data.satsInView, _gnss.getPDOP(), data.hdop);
792	    }
793	
794	    if (_gnss.getDateValid()) {
795	//      M5_LOGD("[%lu] _gnss.getDateValid() is true.", millis());
796	      data.year = _gnss.getYear();
797	      data.month = _gnss.getMonth();
798	      data.day = _gnss.getDay();
799	      data.date_valid = true;
800	      date_timer.restart();
801	      ret_status = e_worker_data_read;
802	    }
803	
804	    if (_gnss.getTimeValid()) {
805	//      M5_LOGD("[%lu] _gnss.getTimeValid() is true.", millis());
806	      data.hour = _gnss.getHour();
807	      data.minute = _gnss.getMinute();
808	      data.second = _gnss.getSecond();
809	      data.time_valid = true;
810	      time_timer.restart();
811	      ret_status = e_worker_data_read;
812	    }
813	  }
814	
815	  // Check expiry
816	  if (location_timer.isExpired()) {
817	    data.location_valid = false;
818	  }
819	  if (time_timer.isExpired()) {
820	    data.time_valid = false;
821	    data.hour = GPS_INVALID_HOUR;
822	    data.minute = GPS_INVALID_MINUTE;
823	    data.second = GPS_INVALID_SECOND;
824	  }
825	  if (date_timer.isExpired()) {
826	    data.date_valid = false;
827	    data.year = GPS_INVALID_YEAR;
828	    data.month = GPS_INVALID_MONTH;
829	    data.day = GPS_INVALID_DAY;
830	  }
831	
832	  ApiDataCache::instance().update(data);
833	  return ret_status;
834	}
